Both **producer** conditions are now clear; what remains is `.github`-side: publish the typed `Fsgg.Registry` validator assertion over `skills.yml`, then flip and update the `skill-mirror-verified` / `fsgg-sdd-orchestrator-axis` cross-refs. The two guards #290's body named — [#288](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/288) and [#289](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/289) — are **both closed**. A green `skill-registry-coherence` on `main` remains a **precondition**: flipping `coherent: true` over a red gate is precisely the fails-open pattern epic #266 exists to kill. That gate drifts on every producer skill-body merge because no propagation edge exists ([.github#299](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/299), open) — it was red again at `df8b69b` while this change was being written — so the flip's owner must re-check it at flip time rather than trust this note. Correcting a stale blocker is not the same as clearing the way.

  **Also corrected while rewriting the note:** the product-row count `12 → 21` (17 Rendering-owned + 4 Game-owned since the ADR-0022 P4 ownership migration, [FS.GG.Game#4](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Game/pull/4)), which had been stale in the note and in the `github → rendering` edge since that migration.

- **2026-07-09** — **`fs-gg-ui-template` package 0.4.0 → 0.4.1: a TEMPLATE-ONLY cut over a HELD 0.4.0 framework pin (publish-before-flip step 2)** (owner rendering; refs [FS.GG.Rendering#242](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/pull/242), [FS.GG.Rendering#235](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/issues/235)). The contract's `version` (the `FsGgUiVersion` framework pin) **does not move**; only `package-version` and `package-tag` do.

  **Why a release was needed at all.** The immediately prior entry moved Rendering's template payload onto stable `FS.GG.Game.Core 0.2.0` + `FS.GG.Audio 0.1.0`. That satisfied nothing a *user* can see: a **scaffold** instantiates the **published** `FS.GG.UI.Template` package, not `main`. `0.4.0` — the newest published template — still carried `0.1.0-preview.1` pins, so `dotnet new fs-gg-ui --profile game` kept generating a **preview-consuming product from a stable template**, which is exactly #235's acceptance. Re-pinning `main` and closing the card would have been the fails-open move: the record says fixed, reality does not. Only a new template package closes it.

  **Why the pin holds.** Only `template/` content moved, so the 17 `FS.GG.UI.*` libraries are byte-identical. They are packed at the pin and pushed `--skip-duplicate` (a no-op); `FS.GG.UI.Canvas` and `.Scene` newest on the org feed both remain `0.4.0` — **verified against the feed, not inferred from intent**. The package version therefore **decouples** from the framework pin, as it did across `0.3.0 → 0.3.1-preview.1` (ADR-0024 step 3) and `0.1.65 → 0.1.70`. Consequently the release cut a **pair** of tags, `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.4.1 → v0.4.1`, and **no `fs-gg-ui/v0.4.1` snapshot**; `validate-version-coherence.fsx`'s `RELEASE-PENDING` block named exactly those two, which is the check that a template-only cut is what actually happened.

  **The gate caught the window.** Between the org-feed push and this row, `scripts/check-feed-coherence.py` reported `fs-gg-ui-template: registry package-version is BEHIND the feed — declares '0.4.0' but ... newest on the org feed is '0.4.1'`. That is [.github#267](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/267) doing the job [.github#250](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/250) went unnoticed for want of — the first time this class was caught by a gate rather than by hand.

  **Dispatch discharged.** `template-dispatch.yml` notified FS.GG.Templates correctly (`upstream-bump` run [29047461324](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Templates/actions/runs/29047461324) → [Templates#122](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Templates/pull/122)), confirming the reserved-`GITHUB_REF` fix in [FS.GG.Rendering#232](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/pull/232) on a real release. The `templates → rendering` **edge** is deliberately **not** moved here: an edge names what the consumer pins, and Templates#122 has not merged. It flips after that lands — the same window this log has recorded twice today.

- **2026-07-09** — **CONSUMER EDGES `rendering → audio` 0.1.0-preview.1 → 0.1.0 and `rendering → game` 0.1.0-preview.1 → 0.2.0: publish-before-flip step 3** (owner rendering; refs [FS.GG.Rendering#238](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/pull/238), [FS.GG.Rendering#235](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/issues/235), [FS.GG.Audio#4](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Audio/issues/4)). No contract row moved — this entry records **consumer pins**, not producer versions.

  **What moved.** Rendering's `template/base/Directory.Packages.props` re-pinned both payload axes onto stable: `<FsGgGameVersion>` `0.1.0-preview.1` → `0.2.0` and `<FsGgAudioVersion>` `0.1.0-preview.1` → `0.1.0`. An edge records what the consumer **actually pins**, so both edges move now and not before.

  **Why the edges lagged their contract rows.** `game-sim-core` has read `0.2.0` since the FS.GG.Game release, and `fs-gg-audio` flipped to `0.1.0` in the PR immediately prior ([.github#279](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/pull/279)). Publish-before-flip deliberately produces this window: producer row first, consumer edge after the re-pin lands. `.github#250` set the precedent, holding `templates → rendering` at `0.2.0` until FS.GG.Templates#119 moved the pin. A contract row and its consuming edge naming different versions **inside that window is correct, not drift** — and #279 said so inline so this edge would not be "corrected" early.

  **What it fixes.** A **stable** `fs-gg-ui` template (`0.4.0`) was scaffolding products that restored **prerelease** `FS.GG.Game.*` and `FS.GG.Audio.*`. Template payload is *content*, not a nuspec dependency, so NU5104 cannot see it and **no gate was ever red** — the fails-open class of epic [#266](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/266). The Game pin was additionally two minors stale on its own axis.

  **Verified by restore, not by inspection.** Rendering's `Package.Tests` read the payload as **text** (they assert axis *structure* and explicitly disclaim the pin's value), so its green CI does **not** prove the new pins resolve. A direct `dotnet restore` of the six pinned packages against nuget.org resolves the full transitive graph to **seven `FS.GG.*` packages with zero prereleases** — including `FS.GG.Game.Render 0.2.0 → FS.GG.UI.Scene 0.4.0`. That is the acceptance of FS.GG.Rendering#235 at the payload level.

  **FS.GG.Rendering#235 stays OPEN.** Its acceptance speaks of a *scaffolded* product, and the payload only reaches consumers when a new `FS.GG.UI.Template` package is published. That needs a template-only `0.4.1` cut (the `0.3.1-preview.1` precedent: package version decouples from a held framework pin), then an `fs-gg-ui-template` `package-version` flip and a `FS.GG.Templates` provider re-pin. Left as an explicit decision — a `main` pin is not a shipped payload.


- **2026-07-09** — **`fs-gg-audio` 0.1.0-preview.1 → 0.1.0 (source + published): the CHANNEL PROMOTION that retires the org's last `-preview` producer** (owner audio; refs [FS.GG.Audio#4](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Audio/issues/4), [FS.GG.Audio#5](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Audio/pull/5), [ADR-0023](../docs/adr/0023-onboard-fs-gg-audio-as-an-sdd-driven-component.md)). Every `FS.GG.*` producer in the org is now on a stable channel.

  **What moved.** The `fs-gg-audio` contract's `version` and `package-version` both advance to `0.1.0`. All four packages — `FS.GG.Audio.Core`/`.Host`/`.Engine`/`.Elmish` — are live on the org feed and on nuget.org at `0.1.0` (release run [29043991855](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Audio/actions/runs/29043991855), tag [`v0.1.0`](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Audio/releases/tag/v0.1.0)). Publish-before-flip (FR-007) observed: the four `Created` responses landed at 19:22Z; this row flipped after.

  **Not an artificial release.** `0.1.0` carries **byte-identical `src/`** to `0.1.0-preview.1` — the last `src/` commit predates the preview tag, and the only commits between the two tags were CI config and the coordination kit. The promotion ships finished bits (Audio's four work items were complete at preview.1), which is why the issue's "no artificial release" condition could never have fired on its own: waiting for content would have waited forever.

  **The `rendering → audio` edge deliberately still reads `0.1.0-preview.1`.** An edge records what the **consumer pins**, and Rendering's `template/base/Directory.Packages.props` has not moved yet. It advances with the re-pin in [FS.GG.Rendering#235](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/issues/235) (publish-before-flip step 3) — the same sequencing `.github#250` used for `templates → rendering`. A contract row and its consuming edge naming different versions is the **correct** intermediate state, not drift.

  **What this now unblocks.** (1) [FS.GG.Rendering#235](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/issues/235) — a *stable* `fs-gg-ui` template still scaffolds a product that restores *prerelease* audio; that pin can finally move to a stable target. (2) The org Renovate preset (`default.json`) held `ignoreUnstable: false` / `respectLatest: false` across **all** `FS.GG.*` solely so Renovate could still see Audio's previews, which meant it proposed prereleases over stable releases org-wide. With Audio stable, that constraint is gone and the preset can be tightened.

  **Gate note.** `check-feed-coherence.py` compares `package-version` against feed-newest and fails on **both** directions. Between the publish and this flip, `.github@main` was genuinely red on `fs-gg-audio` (four `BEHIND the feed` errors) — the daily `17 6 * * *` schedule exists to catch exactly that window, and it worked as designed. This entry closes it.

- **2026-07-09** — **NEW COHERENCE ID `pin-feed-coherence` (coherent: true).** The repo's **embedded, Renovate-annotated version pins** are now gated against the **live org feed**, and the bump mechanism itself is gated (owner github; refs [.github#263](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/263), child of the fails-open epic [.github#266](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/266)). No contract version changed — this entry registers a **gate** and a **root-cause fix**, not a flip. Also annotates `cross-repo-auto-update` and `registry-validator-typed` with what this proves about them.

  **The hole.** `registry-feed-coherence` (the entry below) gates the registry's `package-version` rows against reality. Nothing gated the pins **the gates themselves run on**. `contract-coherence.yml` installs the typed registry validator at a pinned `FS.GG.SDD.Cli` version, and the row `registry-validator-typed` asserts `coherent: true` *on the strength of that pin tracking feed-newest* — because under the registry's additive tolerance a frozen typed validator silently degrades toward a "does the YAML parse" check. The pin froze at `0.2.1` while the org shipped `0.5.0` ([.github#127](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/127), "H2"), was advanced by hand, then froze **again** at `0.5.0` while the org shipped `0.6.0`, `0.7.0` and `0.8.0`. Found by eye during the `v0.9.0` release, three minors late, both times.

  **The root cause** — three hypotheses stood unverified in #263; this is the one that held. `.github`'s own `renovate.json` carried **no feed `hostRules` token**. `FS.GG.*` resolves only from the private org GitHub Packages feed, and Renovate does **not** substitute `{{ secrets }}` inside a preset pulled via `extends`, so the token must live in each repo's **own** config. All four product repos carry it. `.github` — which *authors* the preset and dogfoods it — never adopted its own requirement, on the recorded belief that "the `FS.GG.*` rules are inert here (no `FS.GG.*` PackageReferences)". An annotated **tool pin** is an `FS.GG.*` dependency too. Every `FS.GG.*` lookup therefore 401'd, no versions could be enumerated, and no bump PR could ever open. The symptom was recorded **twice** — *"only third-party Renovate PRs observed so far"*, in this registry and in the fabric guide — and read as a pending milestone rather than a fault; third-party packages resolve from `nuget.org` and need no token, so "PRs, but never for `FS.GG.*`" is precisely the signature of missing feed auth. The annotation manager's regex, the other leading hypothesis, matches the pin correctly (verified).

  **The gate.** `scripts/check-pin-coherence.py`, run by `.github/workflows/pin-coherence.yml` on every PR touching the pins/preset/gate/fixture, on push to `main`, and **daily on a schedule** — load-bearing for the same reason as its sibling: a CLI release that leaves the pin behind touches no file in this repo, which is exactly how three minors passed unseen. It asserts **both** halves of *"this pin can move, and it has moved"*: **freshness** (every annotated pin equals feed-newest, failing **behind** — the freeze — and **ahead** — a version no consumer can restore) and **mechanism** (this repo's Renovate config carries a `hostRules` token for `nuget.pkg.github.com`). Its **subject is the org preset's own annotation regex**, read from `default.json` rather than re-specified, so the gate and the bot cannot disagree about what a pin is: a reformat that blinds the manager also reds the gate.

  **Fails closed**, per the epic's rule. A missing/empty/wrong-host token, no Renovate config, a preset with no annotation manager, **zero pins matched repo-wide**, a pin named in `REQUIRED_PINS` gone invisible to that regex, an unresolvable datasource or non-`FS.GG.*` package, an unreadable feed, a 404, zero versions, or an unparsable literal is an **error**, never a skip. `REQUIRED_PINS` answers the case a scan structurally cannot: a gate that scans with the regex under suspicion sees exactly the nothing the bot sees, so the pins this repo is known to carry are named explicitly and a **disappearance** is red. Versions compare by NuGet **order**, never substring ([.github#268](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/268)). Feed reads and NuGet ordering are now shared with `check-feed-coherence.py` via `scripts/fsgg_feed.py`, so the two gates cannot drift into disagreeing about version order; `feed-coherence.yml` gained that path in its triggers. `tests/pin-coherence/` asserts each failure leg fires for its **stated reason** (the vacuous "must fail" defect the epic names, SDD#299), and is mutation-tested against 7 deliberate fails-open regressions — mechanism check never raises, freshness always OK, required-pin assert no-op, substring compare instead of order, zero-pins tripwire removed, a non-`FS.GG.*` package silently skipped, the `ignorePaths` guard disabled, Renovate's config-resolution order reversed — all caught.

  **Scanning exactly what the bot scans.** The gate skips the paths Renovate's `ignorePaths` excludes (`config:recommended` → `:ignoreModulesAndTests`: `**/tests/**`, `**/vendor/**`, …). Found the hard way: the preset's `managerFilePatterns` include `/\.sh$/`, so the gate's *own fixture* — whose heredocs carry annotation-shaped test pins — reddened its first live run over an `Expecto` pin Renovate would never have touched. A gate stricter than the bot is as wrong as one laxer than it. The converse is guarded by `REQUIRED_PINS`: an operative pin parked under an ignored path reads as *gone invisible to the manager*, which is red.

  **What it does and does not prove.** `registry-validator-typed`'s `coherent: true` is now **earned** rather than assumed. The pin is bot-managed again rather than a release-runbook chore — which is what #263's ask 2 proposed while the mechanism was unproven; enforcement at the point of use beats prose in a runbook. `cross-repo-auto-update` **stays `coherent: false`**: the missing token explains `.github`'s silence, not necessarily the product repos', and no green `FS.GG.*` Renovate sweep has been observed **anywhere**. Verified live at adoption: the one annotated pin (`FS.GG.SDD.Cli` `0.9.0`) equals the feed's newest, and `renovate.json` now carries the token.

- **2026-07-09** — **NEW COHERENCE ID `registry-feed-coherence` (coherent: true).** The registry's `package-version` is now gated against the **live org feed** (owner github; refs [.github#267](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/267), child of the fails-open epic [.github#266](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/266)). No contract version changed — this entry registers a **gate**, not a flip.

  **The hole.** `contract-coherence.yml` validated `dependencies.yml` against its **schema** and its **projection**. Nothing validated it against **reality**: the packages actually served by `nuget.pkg.github.com/FS-GG`. So publish-before-flip (FR-007) step 1 (publish) was gated hard and step 2 (flip) was gated by *nobody noticing*. This was named as the root cause in the [.github#250](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/250) entry below — *"no gate compares the registry's `package-version` against the live feed"* — and then recurred twice: the `fs-gg-ui-template` `0.4.0-preview.1` flip that was never made at all (reconciled in the entry above), and the frozen validator pin ([.github#263](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/263)). Every instance drifted in the **safe** direction (feed ahead of registry), which is luck, not design.

  **The gate.** `scripts/check-feed-coherence.py`, run by `.github/workflows/feed-coherence.yml` on every PR touching the registry, on push to `main`, and **daily on a schedule**. The schedule is the load-bearing trigger: a release that publishes without flipping the registry touches no file in this repo, so no path-filtered trigger can ever see it. Both directions fail, with distinct messages — **behind** the feed (a release published, the registry was never flipped — the observed failure) and **ahead** of it (the registry advertises a version consumers cannot restore — the FR-007 inversion, never yet observed and equally undetected).

  **It fails closed, per the epic's rule.** "Nothing to check" and "checked, and it's fine" must not share an exit code, so an unreadable feed, a 404 package, a missing token, an unrecognised feed response, a zero-version package, an unparsable version literal, or a package-bearing contract with **no mapped package id** is an **error**, never a skip. Versions compare by NuGet **order**, never by substring — the [.github#268](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/268) defect class, in which `0.4.0` matches inside `0.4.0-preview.1` — and the 4-segment grammar (`governance-reference-gate-set` `1.2.1.1`, ADR-0007) is admitted. The gate reads the NuGet **v3 flat-container index**, which is what a `dotnet restore` resolves against, rather than the packages REST API proposed in #267: that endpoint wants a classic PAT with `read:packages` and does not accept the run-scoped `GITHUB_TOKEN`, and it answers *"what does the packages API say"* rather than *"what can a consumer restore"*.

  **Coverage + its own guard.** The six package-bearing contracts (`fsgg-contracts`, `governance-reference-gate-set`, `fs-gg-ui-template`, `game-sim-core`, `game-scene-adapter`, `fs-gg-audio`); `fs-gg-audio`'s four packages are each compared, so a **partial** coherent-set publish is reported rather than hidden behind `.Core`. `fs-gg-ui-template` compares only its **template package** — its `version` is the framework pin and decouples across template-only releases. A new package-bearing contract must be mapped in the gate's `CONTRACT_PACKAGES` table, and forgetting to **fails** the gate rather than silently narrowing its coverage. The fixture (`tests/feed-coherence/`) asserts every failure leg fires *for its stated reason*, so the gate cannot rot into the vacuous "must fail" test this same epic names ([SDD#299](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.SDD/pull/299)); it was mutation-tested against 17 deliberate fails-open regressions, all caught. Two further fails-open surfaces are closed by construction: an **unquoted** `package-version` fails the gate (YAML coerces `1.10` to the float `1.1`, so the literal compared would not be the one written), and the gate's `--fixture` flag — which serves a canned feed — **refuses to run** unless the test harness sets `FSGG_FEED_FIXTURE_OK=1`, so a stray `--fixture` in CI fails rather than quietly turning the gate into a no-op. Verified live at adoption: all six `package-version` literals equal the feed's newest (`1.4.0` / `1.2.1.1` / `0.4.0` / `0.2.0` / `0.2.0` / `0.1.0-preview.1`). `fsgg-sdd registry validate` → valid (0 diagnostics); projection `docs/registry/compatibility.md` updated in the same change.

- **2026-07-09** — **STABLE-CHANNEL TRAIN.** FLIP `fs-gg-ui-template` **`version` 0.3.0-preview.1 → 0.4.0** + **`package-version` 0.3.1-preview.1 → 0.4.0** + **`package-tag` → `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.4.0`**, and FLIP `game-sim-core` + `game-scene-adapter` **0.1.0-preview.1 → 0.2.0** (owner rendering/game; refs FS.GG.Rendering#231, FS.GG.Rendering#232, FS.GG.Game#26, FS.GG.Game#27, release runs [29035865679](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/actions/runs/29035865679) + [29036632410](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Game/actions/runs/29036632410)). The org's first **stable** framework + game sets; both live on the org feed **and** nuget.org, publish-before-flip (FR-007) held. The lines stay **0.x** — no 1.0.

  **Why the two moved together, and in that order.** `FS.GG.Game.Render` reaches up to `FS.GG.UI.Scene` (ADR-0022 §2). NuGet's **NU5104** forbids a stable package from taking a prerelease dependency, and every published `FS.GG.UI.Scene` was a prerelease — so `FS.GG.Game` 0.2.0 could not be cut at all. Its first attempt ([run 29034894859](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Game/actions/runs/29034894859)) died at pack, **before** any push: org feed and nuget.org untouched, the dangling `v0.2.0` tag deleted. Gated ordering (ADR-0012 §4) held. Rendering therefore went first (`0.4.0` stable), Game re-pinned `FS.GG.UI.Scene` `0.1.64-preview.1 → 0.4.0` (FS.GG.Game#27, a two-major jump that builds clean — the removed `Geometry` surface is exactly what `Game.Core` absorbed in ADR-0022), and then `v0.2.0` cut green. **That pin must stay stable**; a `-preview` there fails the release at pack, which no ordinary build surfaces.

  **`fs-gg-ui-template` absorbs TWO flips.** `0.4.0-preview.1` shipped (tags `fs-gg-ui/v0.4.0-preview.1` + `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.4.0-preview.1`, feed serving it) and **the registry was never flipped** — this row still read 0.3.0/0.3.1. That is the **third instance** of the [.github#250](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/250) missing-flip class, after #250 itself and the frozen validator pin (.github#263). Drift was again in the safe direction (feed ahead of registry) and no `coherent:` flag went false. This entry reconciles it and the channel promotion in one move. `0.4.0` stable carries **byte-identical library source** to `0.4.0-preview.1` — SemVer orders the prerelease first, and the only commits between the two tags were docs, CI, and the ApiCompat gate. pin == package-version == `0.4.0`, so this was a **framework** release (17 `FS.GG.UI.*` + BOM republished at the pin, snapshot tag `fs-gg-ui/v0.4.0`), not a template-only cut.

  **The cut exposed a dead dispatch.** `release-tags.yml`'s `workflow_call` path had never cut a real release before — every prior release reached `template-dispatch.yml` through its `push: tags` lane. On `workflow_call` the runner sets `GITHUB_REF` to the **caller's** ref, and `template-dispatch.yml` compensated with `env: GITHUB_REF:` — a name the runner **reserves and re-injects**, so the override was printed in the step log and ignored by the process. The set published to both feeds and `FS.GG.Templates` was never told: *published, unannounced*, precisely the failure `release.yml:308` already warns about. Fixed in [FS.GG.Rendering#232](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Rendering/pull/232) (read `RELEASE_TAG_REF`, a name the runner does not own; three harness scenarios, one of which fails against the old script). The dispatch was then re-fired by re-pushing `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.4.0` at the same SHA, and [FS.GG.Templates#122](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Templates/pull/122) opened.

  **No `coherent:` flip; no `schemaVersion` bump.** The `templates → rendering` **edge deliberately stays at `@0.3.1-preview.1`**: an edge records what the consumer *pins*, and Templates#122 is open, not merged — publish-before-flip **step 3**, exactly as .github#250 and #238 handled it. The `rendering → game` edge likewise stays at `@0.1.0-preview.1`: the template payload still declares `<FsGgGameVersion>0.1.0-preview.1</FsGgGameVersion>` and `<FsGgAudioVersion>0.1.0-preview.1</FsGgAudioVersion>`. Those are **template content, not nuspec dependencies** of any packed library, so they cannot trip NU5104 — but a scaffolded product consumes prerelease Game/Audio until the template re-pins. **`FS.GG.Audio` is now the only producer still on the preview channel**, which is why the org Renovate preset's `ignoreUnstable: false` / `respectLatest: false` (`default.json`) are **left as-is**: flipping them now would stop Renovate proposing the Audio previews the template still needs. `fsgg-sdd registry validate` → valid (0 diagnostics); projection `docs/registry/compatibility.md` updated in the same change.

- **2026-07-09** — SDD release **`v0.9.0`** — `FS.GG.SDD.Cli` **0.8.0 → 0.9.0**; **no contract field moves**; the contract-coherence **gate pin advances 0.5.0 → 0.9.0** (owner sdd/github; refs FS.GG.SDD#299, release run [29033117690](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.SDD/actions/runs/29033117690), ADR-0015, .github#127/H2). The CLI is live on the org feed **and** nuget.org (both push steps green; nuget.org flatcontainer serves `0.9.0`). **`fsgg-contracts` HOLDS at `1.4.0`** — source `<Version>` unchanged, so the release republished the identical package as a `--skip-duplicate` no-op; `version` == `package-version` == source == feed(newest) == **1.4.0** still. No `coherent:` flip, no `schemaVersion` bump, no `minimum-fsgg-sdd` advance (0.6.0 remains the oldest coherent CLI). **The first attempt of this release FAILED and published nothing** ([run 29032490538](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.SDD/actions/runs/29032490538)): `registry validate` confines `<path>` to the workspace and rejects rooted paths (`escapesRoot`, SDD#263), but the C6 self-containment smoke still handed it absolute fixture paths, so the gate went red *before* `Push to the org feed` and both nuget.org steps were skipped. Gated ordering (ADR-0012 §4) held — fail-closed, no half-published set. The smoke also hid a **vacuous failure leg**: its malformed fixture lived in a `/tmp` `mktemp -d`, so the expected non-zero exit came from the path guard rather than from parsing the YAML — it had stopped testing "malformed registry ⇒ exit 1" entirely. Both fixed in [FS.GG.SDD#299](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.SDD/pull/299), which also asserts the failure leg's exit did not come from `PathEscape` so the vacuity cannot return; the `v0.9.0` tag was re-pointed at the fix and re-run green. **H2 RECURRED, caught by hand:** the gate's `FS.GG.SDD.Cli` pin sat at **0.5.0** while the org published 0.6.0, 0.7.0 and 0.8.0 — the exact "typed gate degrades toward a YAML-parses check" defect [.github#127](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/127) closed, reopened by a Renovate annotation-manager bump PR that never arrived. The pin is advanced to 0.9.0 here and its comment now says to treat the literal as manual until the manager is explained; **[`registry-validator-typed`](dependencies.yml) stays `coherent: true`** because the 0.9.0 CLI validates this document clean (`fsgg-sdd registry validate dotgithub/registry/dependencies.yml` → valid, 0 diagnostics — verified against the real file before the pin moved), but the *mechanism* the row depends on is unproven. **Why no `docs/registry/compatibility.md` change:** no registry field moved; the projection's `fsgg-contracts` row already reads 1.4.0 / 1.4.0, and no field tracks the CLI's newest published version (only `fs-gg-ui-template.minimum-fsgg-sdd`, a floor, unchanged).

- **2026-07-08** — NEW dependency edge **`rendering → audio`** (`fs-gg-audio@0.1.0-preview.1`), `fs-gg-audio.consumers` **`[audio] → [audio, rendering]`**, and FLIP `fs-gg-ui-template` **`package-version` 0.3.0 → 0.3.1-preview.1** over a **HELD** `version` (owner rendering/audio; refs .github#238, epic .github#234, ADR-0024 step 3, ADR-0023, FS.GG.Rendering#156 wired / #162 + PR #163 released, release run 28937999798): the **first consumer edge** of the `fs-gg-audio` package contract. The Rendering template's `game`/`sample-pack` profiles now declare `<FsGgAudioVersion>0.1.0-preview.1</FsGgAudioVersion>` and reference `FS.GG.Audio.Core`/`.Host`/`.Engine`/`.Elmish` under the `game || sample-pack` gate — the **real** playback realization, replacing the record-only `fs-gg-audio` *skill* vocabulary that ADR-0024 step 2 retired from `FS.GG.UI.Canvas`. Audio stays a **bottom layer** (depends on no FS-GG component); this is Rendering reaching further *up* to a sibling bottom layer, exactly as it does to `FS.GG.Game.Core`. Templates consumes the audio packages **transitively** through the template — there is no `templates → audio` edge. **`version` HOLDS at 0.3.0-preview.1** while `package-version` advances to `0.3.1`: a **TEMPLATE-ONLY** cut (only `template/` moved), so the 16 `FS.GG.UI.*` libraries are byte-identical, are **not** republished, have **no `fs-gg-ui/v0.3.1` snapshot tag**, and `FS.GG.UI.Canvas` newest on the feed stays `0.3.0` — the same decoupling as `0.1.65`→`0.1.70`. Publish-before-flip (FR-007) held throughout: the audio packages are live on the org feed **and** nuget.org, and `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.3.1-preview.1` shipped, **before** this edge was written. The **`templates → rendering` edge advances `0.2.0` → `0.3.1-preview.1`**, closing the intentional lag [.github#250](https://github.com/FS-GG/.github/issues/250) recorded: that edge names what the consumer *pins*, and `FS.GG.Templates` only re-pinned in [FS.GG.Templates#119](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Templates/pull/119) (publish-before-flip **step 3**). `0.3.0` was never pinned by Templates — `0.3.1` superseded it before the re-pin landed. **No `coherent:` flip; no `schemaVersion` bump** (additive edge + version advance). `docs/architecture.md` is reconciled in the same change: its §5 sentence *"Rendering points at nothing"* and the diagram's *"Rendering still reaches up to nothing"* were **already false** (the `rendering → game` edge, ADR-0022 P5) and this edge made them doubly so; the §5 contract table gained the missing `game-sim-core`, `game-scene-adapter`, and `fs-gg-audio` rows. `fsgg-sdd registry validate` → valid; projection `docs/registry/compatibility.md` updated in the same change.

- **2026-07-08** — FLIP `fs-gg-ui-template` **0.2.0-preview.1 → 0.3.0-preview.1** — a LATE flip of a release that shipped 2026-07-07 (owner rendering; refs .github#250, ADR-0024 step 2, FS.GG.Rendering#158, release PR FS.GG.Rendering#159 `4217886`, release run 28882426507): the **FRAMEWORK MAJOR** that **retired the Audio request surface** (`AudioEffect` / `SoundId` / `TrackId` / the `Audio.*` module) from `FS.GG.UI.Canvas`. That vocabulary now lives only in the standalone `FS.GG.Audio.Core` (`fs-gg-audio` **0.1.0-preview.1**, ADR-0023) — a **breaking removal** in a shipped library; the ApiCompat gate passed it against the 0.2.0 baseline. `Canvas.Audio` was a **module inside** `FS.GG.UI.Canvas`, never its own package, so nothing was unlisted. Advances `version` (the template's `<FsGgUiVersion>` framework pin), `package-version`, and `package-tag` → `fs-gg-ui-template/v0.3.0-preview.1`; tags `v0.3.0-preview.1` + `fs-gg-ui/v0.3.0-preview.1` snapshot the 16 `FS.GG.UI.*` libs, live on the org feed **and** nuget.org. Pin == package == release tag == 0.3.0 (Feature-209). **Why this entry exists at all:** publish-before-flip (FR-007) ran only half — the packages went live 2026-07-07T16:38Z and the registry was never flipped, so `dependencies.yml` advertised 0.2.0 for a day while the feed served 0.3.0. Two registry PRs (.github#246, #248) landed on top of the stale row without noticing. The drift was in the **safe** direction (feed AHEAD of registry, not the FR-007 inversion where the registry names a version the feed cannot serve) and `coherent: true` never became false — this was a **missing flip, not a broken set**. **No `coherent:` flip; no `schemaVersion` bump.** The `templates → rendering` **edge deliberately stays at `fs-gg-ui-template@0.2.0-preview.1`**: an edge records what the consumer *pins*, and `FS.GG.Templates` `providers/rendering.providers.yml` still pins `FS.GG.UI.Template::0.2.0-preview.1`. The re-pin (publish-before-flip step 3) is deferred to .github#238, which moves the pin and this edge together to `0.3.1-preview.1` once FS.GG.Rendering#162 ships the FS.GG.Audio template edit. **Root cause of the miss:** no gate compares the registry's `package-version` against the live feed — `contract-coherence` validates the registry against its schema and its projection, not against reality. `fsgg-sdd registry validate` → valid (0 diagnostics); projection `docs/registry/compatibility.md` updated in the same change.

- SCAFFOLD-PROVENANCE 1.0.0→1.1.0 (owner sdd; ADR-0014 P0.D0.2, FS.GG.SDD#60): the `scaffold-provenance` contract gains an ADDITIVE optional per-path `sha256` on the produced/mirrored path objects — the content-addressed half of ADR-0014 §Decision 3. FS.GG.Contracts (unpublished; the coherent CLI/Contracts release is ADR-0014 P1) defines the shapes: SkillManifest ({id,scope:process|product,sha256,body|resolvablePath}) + the AGENT_SKILL_ROOTS constant (.claude/.codex/.agents) + ScaffoldProducedPathEntry.Sha256, package contract 1.2.0→1.3.0. The runtime emitter OMITS `sha256` when absent so current output stays BYTE-IDENTICAL and the in-code schemaVersion stays 1 (digest population is P1). ADDITIVE/backward-compatible: a 1.0.0 consumer ignores the unknown field (typed validator stays additive-tolerant, SDD#32/#49). No coherence flag flips — `skill-mirror-verified` STAYS coherent:false (flips true in P4). Contract touched: scaffold-provenance (minor, +sha256).

- SKILL-VENDORING ROBUSTNESS REGISTERED (owner github; ADR-0014, extends ADR-0011): new coherence id `skill-mirror-verified` (coherent:false) — a four-repo audit found the skill-mirror satisfies ADR-0011's invariants via FOUR hand-maintained "materialize union → 3 roots" implementations (SDD scaffold/refresh/doctor + the fs-gg-ui template's Feature 230 36-twin standalone matrix) with NO content verification (doctor/composition check presence only; scaffold-provenance has no digest, so ADR-0011's sha256 rationale is counterfactual) and ~13 dangling dev-surface wrappers shipped into products. ADR-0014 consolidates to ONE content-addressed algorithm: a per-producer skill-manifest ({id,scope,sha256,body}) + one mirror/verify library in FS.GG.Contracts (root-set parameterized via AGENT_SKILL_ROOTS) consumed by both lanes (orchestrated fsgg-sdd + standalone template single-materialize), scaffold-provenance gains per-skill sha256 (additive minor), doctor+composition assert present∧byte-identical-across-roots∧matches-manifest for process∧product skills, and a product/dev boundary + no-dangling guard remove the wrappers. Phased/publish-before-flip/additive (P0 contract → P1 SDD library+content-drift → P2 Rendering manifest+single-materialize → P3 .github assertion + Templates composition-asserts → P4 flip enforcing+re-pin+delete residual, superseding the #47 lockstep). Roadmap: docs/reports/2026-07-01-skill-vendoring-robustness-roadmap.md. Contract touched: scaffold-provenance (minor, +sha256), fs-gg-ui-template, scaffold-provider. No coherence flag flips true→false (new id only).
